- **exploratory-bug-bash**: Advisory AI exploratory testing (tester-army/e2e `explore`) against an isolated stack: LiteLLM actor/judge routing, bounded budgets, planted-bug calibration, and deterministic Playwright reproduction before any finding is confirmed. - **exploratory-bug-hunt**: Skill para caça proativa de bugs e gaps sem pistas iniciais; gera relatório versionado e aplica correções. v2.0: 10 categorias de inspeção obrigatórias, checklists técnicos e grep-first approach. - **exploratory-bug-hunt**: Agent-driven bug hunt across the whole OpenCloud web UI (not tied to a changelog). Parallel subagents explore one area each, guided by the web source code, deliberately break flows (cancel, back/forward, slow or failing requests, other roles), then a second round verifies every candidate. Produces release-testing/exploratory/releases/hunt-&lt;date&gt;/report.md. Use for /exploratory-bug-hunt or "hunt for bugs in the web UI".
